class op129_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 8, p1: 1024}
    index0 = 1024*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('buf125', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf122', get_index_1)
        add = ops.add(load, load_1)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf126', get_index_2)
        sub = ops.sub(add, load_2)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')

        store = ops.store('buf129', get_index_4, mul, None)
        return store
